TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上分析了链式思维（CoT）生成如何与上下文强化学习（ICRL）相互作用，证明在策略评估设置下，CoT生成过程等价于重复执行时序差分学习更新，其误差随CoT长度几何级衰减并最终饱和于统计下限，且所需的Transformer参数是预训练损失的全局最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验研究表明CoT能增强ICRL能力，但缺乏理论上的理解，特别是CoT与ICRL如何相互作用的机理尚不明确。

Method: 在策略评估设置下使用线性Transformer进行分析，通过理论推导证明特定Transformer参数下CoT生成过程等价于重复执行TD学习更新，并给出有限样本收敛分析。

Result: CoT生成过程等价于重复执行时序差分学习更新；策略评估误差随CoT长度几何级衰减，最终饱和于由上下文长度决定的统计下限；所需Transformer参数是预训练损失的全局最小值。

Conclusion: 本文提供了CoT增强ICRL能力的理论基础，证明CoT通过模拟TD更新来实现上下文中的策略评估，为理解大型语言模型中的涌现能力提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) refers to the ability of RL agents to adapt to new tasks at inference time without parameter updates by conditioning on additional context. Recent empirical studies further demonstrate that Chain-of-Thought (CoT) generation can amplify this ICRL capability. This paper is the first to provide a theoretical understanding on how CoT interacts with ICRL. We conduct our analysis in a policy evaluation setup with linear Transformer. We prove that with specific Transformer parameters, the CoT generation process is equivalent to repeatedly executing temporal difference learning updates. Additionally, we provide finite sample convergence analysis showing that the policy evaluation error decreases geometrically with CoT length and eventually saturates at a statistical floor determined by the context length. We also prove that the desired Transformer parameters are a global minimizer of the pretraining loss, providing a theoretical understanding on the empirical emergence of those parameters.

</details>


### [76] [The Position Curse: LLMs Struggle to Locate the Last Few Items in a List](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07127)
*Zhanqi Zhang,Hua-Dong Xiong,Robert C. Wilson,Mikio Aoi,Marcelo G. Mattar,Li Ji-An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在"千篇寻一"（从海量无关token中定位单个相关事实）任务上近乎饱和，但在短列表中检索最后几项时却频繁失败，这种现象被称为"位置诅咒"。研究通过前向/后向检索实验发现后向检索显著落后，LoRA微调虽有提升但远未饱和，表明位置检索能力是未来模型设计的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM在位置检索任务中的非对称性失败模式（前向检索好而后向检索差），并为后续预训练目标优化提供依据。

Method: 设计两类互补查询：给定位置（前向/后向偏移）检索对应元素；给定元素返回其位置。构建PosBench位置聚焦训练数据集，进行LoRA微调实验。

Result: 后向检索性能显著低于前向检索；LoRA微调可同时提升前向和后向检索能力，并泛化到代码理解基准PyIndex，但绝对性能仍远未饱和。

Conclusion: 位置检索能力是当前LLM的关键短板，尤其影响代码理解与编辑场景，应成为未来预训练目标和模型设计的重点方向。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) can find a needle in a haystack (locating a single relevant fact buried among hundreds of thousands of irrelevant tokens) with near-saturated accuracy, yet fail to retrieve the last few items in a short list. We call this failure the Position Curse. For instance, even in a two-line code snippet, Claude Opus 4.6 misidentifies the second-to-last line most of the time. To characterize this failure, we evaluated two complementary queries: given a position in a sequence (of letters or words), retrieve the corresponding item; and given an item, return its position. Each position is specified as a forward or backward offset from an anchor, either an endpoint of the list (its start or end) or another item in the list. Across both open-source and frontier closed-source models, backward retrieval substantially lags forward retrieval. To test whether this capability can be rescued by post-training, we constructed PosBench, a position-focused training dataset. LoRA fine-tuning improves both forward and backward retrieval and generalizes to a held-out code-understanding benchmark (PyIndex), yet absolute performance remains far from saturated. As LLM coding agents increasingly operate over large codebases where precise indexing becomes essential for code understanding and editing, position-based retrieval emerges as a key capability for future pretraining objectives and model design.

</details>


### [77] [Simple KNN-Based Outlier Detection Achieves Robust Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07130)
*Tianle Jiang,Yufa Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在最优簇大小的合理假设下，简单移除具有最大K近邻距离的点，即可在鲁棒k-Means问题中实现与现有复杂方法相当的近似保证，且无需引入额外中心或丢弃多余离群点。实验表明该方法在聚类代价和运行时间上优于或匹配多种更复杂的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒k-Means（含离群点的k-Means）在实际应用中至关重要，但经典离群点检测启发式方法在该问题上的有效性的理论和实证理解仍然有限。本文旨在弥合离群点检测与鲁棒聚类之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 在最优簇大小满足一定假设的条件下，提出基于K近邻距离的简单离群点移除策略：移除具有最大K近邻距离的z个点，将鲁棒k-Means问题转化为标准k-Means问题，并证明该策略可取得常数因子近似保证。

Result: 理论上证明该方法可实现从鲁棒k-Means到标准k-Means的常数因子约简，无需引入额外中心或丢弃多余离群点；实验上在真实数据集上表现出优于或匹配多种更复杂算法的聚类代价和运行时间。

Conclusion: 简单的KNN启发式方法在鲁棒聚类中可取得令人惊讶的有效性，为桥接离群点检测与聚类技术提供了新机遇。

Abstract: Being robust to the presence of outliers is crucial for applying clustering algorithms in practice. In the $\textit{robust $k$-Means}$ problem (i.e., $k$-Means with outliers), the goal is to remove $z$ outliers and minimize the $k$-Means cost on the remaining points. Despite the close connection between robust $k$-Means and outlier detection, both theoretical and empirical understanding of the effectiveness of $\textit{classic outlier detection heuristics}$ for robust $k$-Means remains limited. In this paper, we prove that under a practical assumption on the optimal cluster sizes, simply removing points with large $K$-Nearest-Neighbor distances achieves performance comparable to prior work in terms of approximation guarantees: it yields a constant-factor reduction from robust $k$-Means to standard $k$-Means, without introducing additional centers or discarding extra outliers, as is commonly required by existing approaches. Empirically, experiments on real-world datasets show that our method outperforms or matches several more sophisticated algorithms in terms of clustering cost and runtime. These results demonstrate that simple KNN-based heuristics can be surprisingly effective for robust clustering, highlighting new opportunities to bridge techniques from outlier detection and clustering.

</details>


### [78] [GAD in the Wild: Benchmarking Graph Anomaly Detection under Realistic Deployment Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07133)
*Jingjing Zhou,Shiyu Huang,Qing Qing,Zuquan Yuan,Huafei Huang,Ziqi Xu,Mingliang Hou,Xikun Zhang,Renqiang Luo,Ivan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向图异常检测（GAD）的多维度基准测试，系统评估了GAD模型在百万级图、极端异常稀疏和缺失节点属性三种实际部署挑战下的表现。实验发现：GNN方法难以扩展到百万节点图，异常检测性能在现实异常比（如0.1%）下急剧下降，重建模型对属性填补策略高度敏感。实验室表现优异不代表生产环境鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GAD基准局限于小规模、异常比例较均衡的图数据，学术评估与实际部署之间存在巨大差距，缺乏对大规模图、极端异常稀疏和属性缺失等真实场景挑战的系统评估。

Method: 从5个多样化的图（含两个超过370万节点的工业级数据集）中衍生出受控基准变体，对9种代表性GAD模型进行多维度系统评估，涵盖百万级图规模、极端异常稀缺性（如0.1%）和缺失节点属性三大挑战。

Result: (1) 多数GNN方法因内存需求过大无法扩展到百万节点图；(2) 真实异常比（如0.1%）下检测性能骤降，常出现零召回率；(3) 重建模型对属性填补策略高度敏感。

Conclusion: 实验室环境下的强性能无法保证生产环境的鲁棒性，需推动面向大规模、不完美图的鲁棒可扩展GAD系统发展。基准和评估结果已开源作为诊断测试平台。

Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) is a critical task in graph machine learning with vital applications in financial fraud detection and social platform governance. However, existing GAD benchmarks are often restricted to small-scale, curated graphs with relatively balanced anomaly ratios, leaving a substantial gap between academic evaluation and real-world deployment. To bridge this gap, we present a multi-dimensional benchmark that systematically evaluates GAD models under three deployment-relevant challenges: million-scale graphs, extreme anomaly scarcity, and missing node attributes. We derive a family of controlled benchmark variants from five diverse graphs, including two native industrial-scale datasets with over 3.7 million nodes. Our extensive evaluation of nine representative GAD models reveals three major limitations: (1) most GNN-based methods fail to scale to million-node graphs due to prohibitive memory requirements; (2) detection performance drops sharply under realistic anomaly ratios (e.g., 0.1\%), often resulting in zero recall; and (3) reconstruction-based models are highly sensitive to attribute imputation strategies. Our findings suggest that strong performance in laboratory settings does not guarantee robustness in production environments. We release this benchmark and empirical evaluation as a diagnostic testbed to promote the development of robust and scalable GAD systems for large-scale, imperfect graphs encountered in practice. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Benchmark_GAD-E7A3.

</details>


### [79] [Adaptive Negative Reinforcement for LLM Reasoning:Dynamically Balancing Correction and Diversity in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07137)
*Yash Ingle,Jaival Chauhan,Ankit Yadav,Sudhakar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种改进的负样本强化学习方法（A-NSR和CW-NSR），通过自适应惩罚调度和置信度加权机制，提升大语言模型数学推理能力，并在MATH、AIME 2025等数据集上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NSR方法在训练过程中使用固定的惩罚策略，对所有错误响应赋予相同权重，未能区分错误的重要程度，限制了模型推理能力的进一步提升。

Method: 提出两种扩展：A-NSR使用时间依赖调度函数，初始阶段侧重纠错稳定模型，后期转向更精细的更新；CW-NSR根据模型归一化序列似然度分配不同惩罚权重，高置信度错误给予更大惩罚，不确定性错误则减轻惩罚。

Result: 在MATH、AIME 2025、AMC23等困难推理数据集上，使用Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B架构进行评估，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的A-NSR和CW-NSR机制能够实现先验引导的概率重分布，同时天然具备防过拟合能力，有效提升了LLM的推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a highly effective method for improving the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Recent research shows that Negative Sample Reinforcement (NSR) -- which focuses on penalizing incorrect steps rather than simply rewarding correct ones -- can match or even exceed the performance of more complex frameworks like PPO and GRPO across the entire Pass@k spectrum. However, current NSR techniques usually apply a fixed penalty throughout the training process and treat every incorrect response with the same weight.
  To address these limitations, we propose two extensions to the NSR framework: Adaptive Negative Sample Reinforcement. Rather than using a fixed update rule, A-NSR uses time-dependent scheduling functions. In the initial training phases, the system focuses heavily on correcting errors to stabilize the model. As training continues, it shifts toward more subtle and controlled updates. We also introduce Confidence-Weighted Negative Reinforcement, which operates on the principle that different mistakes carry different levels of importance. CW-NSR assigns specific penalty weights based on the model's normalized sequence likelihood. If the model is highly confident in a wrong path, it receives a larger penalty and for uncertain errors -- where the model is effectively exploring -- are penalized less strictly. Our formal analysis shows how these mechanisms govern token-level updates, allowing the model to leverage prior-guided probability redistribution while providing a natural defense against overfitting. We evaluated these methods on difficult reasoning datasets, including MATH, AIME 2025, and AMC23, using the Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B architecture.

</details>


### [80] [Regret-Oracle Complexity Tradeoffs in Agnostic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07155)
*Idan Attias,Steve Hanneke,Arvind Ramaswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应动态的agnostic-to-realizable归约方法，利用弱一致性预言机（仅判断数据集是否可实现）将在线学习的预言机复杂度从双指数级 $O(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}})$ 显著降低至 $O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$，同时保持近最优的期望遗憾，并形式化了遗憾-预言机复杂度的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的agnostic在线学习通过Littlestone's SOA归约为realizable设置，但SOA计算上不可行。现有预言机高效方法虽能达到近最优期望遗憾，却面临双指数级的预言机复杂度，严重限制了实际可用性。本文旨在大幅降低这一复杂度，同时使用更弱的预言机。

Method: 提出自适应动态的agnostic-to-realizable归约方法，核心是主动在线剪枝不可实现的标签序列，利用VC维界动态维护的活跃路径数量，使用弱一致性预言机（仅判断数据集是否可实现）替代ERM预言机。

Result: 预言机查询复杂度从 $O(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}})$ 降至 $O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$，同时保持近最优期望遗憾；证明了遗憾-预言机复杂度的权衡上界和下界：限制 $Q = o(\sqrt{T})$ 次查询时，期望遗憾下界为 $\Omega(T/Q)$。

Conclusion: 通过动态剪枝策略，本文在更弱的预言机假设下实现了预言机复杂度的指数级改进，为agnostic在线学习的实际部署提供了更可行的方案，并系统刻画了查询预算与学习性能之间的基本权衡。

Abstract: Agnostic online learning is classically solved via a reduction to the realizable setting, utilizing Littlestone's Standard Optimal Algorithm (SOA) as a base learner. However, the SOA is computationally intractable to execute even for a single round. To overcome this barrier, recent work in oracle-efficient online learning replaces the SOA with a realizable base learner that accesses the concept class exclusively through an offline empirical risk minimization (ERM) oracle. While such agnostic learners achieve near-optimal expected regret, they suffer from a doubly-exponential oracle complexity of $O\big(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}}\big)$, where $d_\mathrm{LD}$ is the Littlestone dimension and $T$ is the number of rounds. In this work, we significantly improve this oracle complexity while relying on an even weaker primitive: a weak-consistency oracle, which merely decides whether a given labeled dataset is realizable. At the core of our approach is an adaptive and dynamic agnostic-to-realizable reduction that actively prunes non-realizable label sequences on the fly. By using the VC dimension ($d_\mathrm{VC}$) to bound the number of dynamically maintained active paths, our algorithm reduces the total query complexity down to $O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$ while perfectly preserving near-optimal expected regret. Crucially, this dynamic pruning also yields a memory reduction over the standard reduction. Furthermore, we formally quantify the regret--oracle complexity tradeoff, providing upper bounds that smoothly interpolate between restricted query budgets and attainable expected regret. We complement these with lower bounds proving that any learner restricted to $Q = o(\sqrt{T})$ queries must suffer an expected regret of $Ω(T/Q)$.

</details>


### [81] [Cost-Ordered Feasibility for Multi-Armed Bandits with Cost Subsidy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07171)
*Ishank Juneja,Carlee Joe-Wong,Osman Yağan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带成本补贴的多臂老虎机（MAB-CS）问题，其中目标是在满足未知最优奖励约束下最小化累积成本。提出了成本有序可行性（COF）算法，给出了实例相关的下界和上界，并在真实数据集和合成数据上验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中（如推荐系统、资源分配）往往需要在保证最低奖励质量的前提下最小化成本，传统MAB只追求最大奖励无法建模这类约束优化问题。本文希望为这种带质量约束的成本最小化问题提供严谨的理论分析和高效算法。

Method: 首先推导了实例相关的下界，刻画任何策略所需的期望次优样本数。然后提出COF（Cost-Ordered Feasibility）算法，核心思想是根据成本排序，通过智能组合所有臂的样本来评估廉价臂的可行性（是否满足相对于未知最优奖励的质量约束）。最后给出COF的实例相关上界分析。

Result: 推导了严格推广先前界限的实例相关下界；证明了COF的期望累积成本和质量遗憾的实例相关上界；在MovieLens、Goodreads数据集及合成数据上，COF在经验性能上显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上取得了性质更优的遗憾上界，且COF算法在经验性能上也有令人信服的改进，为带约束的MAB问题提供了新的理论洞见和实用算法。

Abstract: The classic multi-armed bandit (MAB) problem tackles the challenge of accruing maximum reward while making decisions under uncertainty. However, in applications, often the goal is to minimize cost subject to a constraint on the minimum permissible reward, an objective captured by multi-armed bandits with cost-subsidy (MAB-CS). Of interest to this paper is the setting where the quality (reward) constraint is specified relative to the unknown best reward and the cost of each arm is known. We characterize the expected sub-optimal samples required by any policy by proving instance-dependent lower bounds that offer new insight into the problem and are a strict generalization of prior bounds. Then, we propose an algorithm called Cost-Ordered Feasibility (COF) that leverages our insight and intelligently combine samples from all arms to gauge the feasibility of a cheap arm. Thereafter, we analyze COF to establish instance-dependent upper bounds on its expected cumulative cost and quality regret, i.e., relative to the cheapest feasible arm. Finally, we empirically validate the merits of COF, comparing it to baselines from the literature through extensive simulation experiments on the MovieLens and Goodreads datasets as well as representative synthetic instances. Not only does our paper develop qualitatively better theoretical regret upper bounds, but COF also convincingly demonstrates improved empirical performance.

</details>


### [82] [HyperEyes: Dual-Grained Efficiency-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Parallel Multimodal Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07177)
*Guankai Li,Jiabin Chen,Yi Xu,Xichen Zhang,Yuan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperEyes，一种并行多模态搜索智能体，通过将视觉定位与检索融合为单一原子动作，支持单轮并发搜索多个实体。采用两阶段训练：冷启动数据合成管道生成并行化训练数据；双粒度效率感知强化学习框架（宏观TRACE奖励抑制冗余工具调用，微观策略内蒸馏注入密集校正信号）。同时引入效率-精度联合评估基准IMEB。在六个基准上，HyperEyes-30B相比最强开源模型准确率提升9.9%，工具调用轮次减少5.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态搜索智能体顺序处理目标实体，每次仅发起一个工具调用，当查询分解为独立子检索时累积大量冗余交互轮次。本文认为有效多模态智能体应"搜索更广而非更长"：在一轮内并发调度多个有依据的查询。

Method: 两阶段训练：(1) 并行友好数据合成管道（Parallel-Amenable Data Synthesis Pipeline），通过渐进式拒绝采样生成覆盖视觉多实体和文本多约束查询的效率导向轨迹；(2) 双粒度效率感知强化学习框架（Dual-Grained Efficiency-Aware RL）：宏观层TRACE（Tool-use Reference-Adaptive Cost Efficiency）以单调收紧的参考线抑制冗余工具调用；微观层策略内蒸馏（On-Policy Distillation）注入来自外部教师模型的密集令牌级校正信号。

Result: 在六个基准上，HyperEyes-30B相比最强可比开源智能体，准确率提升9.9%，工具调用轮次平均减少5.3倍。

Conclusion: 并行多模态搜索策略能显著提升效率，双粒度效率感知强化学习可有效平衡搜索能力与推理成本。IMEB基准填补了联合评估精度与效率的空白。

Abstract: Existing multimodal search agents process target entities sequentially, issuing one tool call per entity and accumulating redundant interaction rounds whenever a query decomposes into independent sub-retrievals. We argue that effective multimodal agents should search wider rather than longer: dispatching multiple grounded queries concurrently within a round. To this end, we present HyperEyes, a parallel multimodal search agent that fuses visual grounding and retrieval into a single atomic action, enabling concurrent search across multiple entities while treating inference efficiency as a first-class training objective. HyperEyes is trained in two stages. For cold-start supervision, we develop a Parallel-Amenable Data Synthesis Pipeline covering visual multi-entity and textual multi-constraint queries, curating efficiency-oriented trajectories via Progressive Rejection Sampling. Building on this, our central contribution, a Dual-Grained Efficiency-Aware Reinforcement Learning framework, operates at two levels. At the macro level, we propose TRACE (Tool-use Reference-Adaptive Cost Efficiency), a trajectory-level reward whose reference is monotonically tightened during training to suppress superfluous tool calls without restricting genuine multi-hop search. At the micro level, we adapt On-Policy Distillation to inject dense token-level corrective signals from an external teacher on failed rollouts, mitigating the credit-assignment deficiency of sparse outcome rewards. Since existing benchmarks evaluate accuracy as the sole metric, omitting inference cost, we introduce IMEB, a human-curated benchmark of 300 instances that jointly evaluates search capability and efficiency. Across six benchmarks, HyperEyes-30B surpasses the strongest comparable open-source agent by 9.9% in accuracy with 5.3x fewer tool-call rounds on average.

</details>


### [83] [Coupling Models for One-Step Discrete Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07193)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Avishek Joey Bose,Anru R. Zhang,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出耦合模型（Coupling Models），一种一步式离散生成模型，通过学习离散序列与高斯潜变量之间的直接耦合，实现单步生成，在文本、生物序列和图像二值化等任务上显著优于现有一步式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散结构上的生成模型大多依赖自回归解码或迭代精炼，生成速度慢且计算成本高。现有蒸馏方法需要预训练多步采样器再压缩，过程复杂。本文旨在设计一种无需预训练蒸馏、可一步完成离散生成的高效模型。

Method: 提出Coupling Models，学习离散序列与高斯潜变量之间的直接耦合关系，训练一个专用的解码器来反转该耦合，从而在单步中生成样本。模型避免了在单纯形上构建复杂连续流，也无需手工指定数据到噪声的耦合方式。

Result: 在LM1B文本生成任务上，困惑度降低33%；在果蝇脑增强子设计任务（Fly Brain）上，FBD降低18%；在MNIST-Binary上，FID降低46%。

Conclusion: 有效的一步式离散生成高度依赖于数据与噪声在解码之前的耦合方式，耦合模型证明通过精心设计的直接耦合可以大幅提升生成质量。

Abstract: Generative modeling over discrete structures underpins applications across deep learning, from biological sequence design and code generation to large language models, yet generation often remains sequential, relying on autoregressive decoding or iterative refinement. In this work, we introduce Coupling Models(Coupling Models), a one-step discrete generative model that learns a direct coupling between discrete sequences and Gaussian latents. Unlike recent distillation methods that compress a pretrained multi-step sampler into a few steps, Coupling Model trains a purpose-built decoder to invert this coupling and generate samples in a single step. The model also avoids complex continuous flows over the simplex and hand-specified data-to-noise couplings. Empirically,Coupling Model improves the strongest one-step baselines in each domain: it reduces LM1B text-generation perplexity by 33% at its lowest-perplexity operating point, Fly Brain enhancer-design FBD by 18%, and MNIST-Binary FID by 46%. These results suggest that effective one-step discrete generation depends strongly on how data and noise are coupled before decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/Coupling-Models.

</details>


### [84] [Arrow: A Foundation Model for Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07204)
*Ryan Thompson,He Zhao,Daniel M. Steinberg,Edwin V. Bonilla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Arrow，一个基于Transformer的零样本因果发现基础模型，通过骨架-拓扑顺序分解保证无环性，在合成数据上监督训练后可直接应用于新数据集，性能优异且推理成本低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法通常需要针对每个新数据集从头运行，计算成本高且难以复用，缺乏一个通用的、可零样本迁移的因果发现模型。

Method: 将DAG分解为无向骨架和拓扑顺序，利用Transformer架构对变量进行上下文编码，分别预测骨架边概率和节点顺序分数，通过端到端可微的有向边复合似然进行监督训练。

Result: 在分布内/外合成数据、半合成数据和真实数据集上，Arrow匹配或超越现有方法，且推理成本显著低于有竞争力的基线。

Conclusion: 大规模多样化合成数据上的预训练可以产生快速、准确且可复用的零样本因果发现模型。

Abstract: We introduce Arrow, a foundation model for zero-shot causal discovery on observational tabular data. Arrow factorizes a directed acyclic graph into an undirected skeleton and a topological order, guaranteeing acyclicity by construction. Given a new dataset, it uses a transformer-based architecture to contextualize variables within and across observations, then predicts skeleton edge probabilities and node order scores that together define a graph. Arrow is trained in a supervised fashion on synthetic datasets with ground-truth graphs, using an end-to-end differentiable directed edge composite likelihood induced by the skeleton-order factorization. The training distribution spans diverse graph families, functional forms, noise models, and dataset shapes. Across in- and out-of-distribution synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real datasets, Arrow matches or outperforms existing causal discovery methods at substantially lower inference cost than competitive alternatives. Our results demonstrate that large-scale pretraining on diverse synthetic data can yield zero-shot causal discovery models that are fast, accurate, and reusable on new datasets.

</details>


### [85] [FAME: Forecasting Academic Impact via Continuous-Time Manifold Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07208)
*Jianrong Ding,Jianyuan Zhong,Zhengyan Shi,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FAME框架，通过连续时间流形演化建模科学主题的动态轨迹，在3200篇arXiv论文上显著优于当前最先进的LLM评估器，实现前瞻性多维度影响力预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于评估研究想法，但由于新想法的真正影响需要多年才能显现，评估此类判断存在根本性困难。本文通过将人类手稿的影响力预测作为可验证的替代任务，发现前沿LLM无法可靠地区分高影响力论文与普通出版物，表明静态文本判断不足以进行科学评估。

Method: 提出FAME（Forecasting Academic Impact via Continuous-Time Manifold Evolution）框架，这是一个时空建模框架，将论文投影到由文本特征和已验证知识流图驱动的动态潜空间中，学习使高影响力手稿与其领域前进动量对齐的几何约束。

Result: 在来自三个快速演化子领域的3200篇arXiv论文上的实验表明，FAME在前瞻性多维度影响力预测中始终且显著优于最先进的LLM评估器。将FAME的动态几何信号整合到LLM中能显著提高其预测性能。

Conclusion: 论文手稿影响力预测可作为有用且可测量的替代基准，FAME有望成为强健的、轨迹感知的自动化科学评估基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to brainstorm and evaluate research ideas, yet assessing such judgments is fundamentally difficult because the true impact of a new idea may take years to emerge. We address this challenge by using the impact forecasting of human-authored manuscripts as a verifiable proxy task. In a prospective forecasting study, we find that frontier LLMs fail to reliably distinguish high-impact papers from ordinary publications, suggesting that static text-based judging is insufficient for scientific evaluation. To address this limitation, we propose $\textbf{FAME}$ ($\underline{\text{F}}$orecasting $\underline{\text{A}}$cademic Impact via Continuous-Time $\underline{\text{M}}$anifold $\underline{\text{E}}$volution), a spatiotemporal framework for modeling the dynamic trajectories of scientific topics. FAME projects papers into a dynamic latent space informed by textual features and a verified knowledge-flow graph, learning geometric constraints that align impactful manuscripts with the forward momentum of their fields. Experiments on 3,200 arXiv papers across three fast-evolving subfields show that FAME consistently and substantially outperforms state-of-the-art LLM evaluators in prospective multidimensional impact forecasting. Furthermore, integrating FAME's dynamic geometric signals into LLMs significantly improves their forecasting performance. These results support manuscript impact forecasting as a useful, measurable proxy benchmark and position FAME as a strong, trajectory-aware foundation for automated scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [86] [HARMONY: Bridging the Personalization-Generalization Gap by Mitigating Representation Skew in Heterogeneous Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07211)
*Jiseok Youn,You Rim Choi,Goodsol Lee,Sangtae Ha,Hyung-Sin Kim,Saewoong Bahk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARMONY提出首个支持异构客户端架构的混合拆分联邦学习框架，通过元学习模拟多样特征提取器、对比学习对齐特征表示，在不牺牲个性化且不共享原始标签的情况下，显著提升了非独立同分布数据下本地分类与远程异常检测的准确率（最高提升43.0%/28.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合拆分联邦学习在客户端架构异构场景下存在表示偏斜（representation skew）问题，即来自不同定制特征提取器的特征无法在共享空间中对齐，导致负责异常检测（OOD）的服务器端模型性能严重下降。

Method: 修改元学习以模拟不同参数和架构下的多样化特征提取器并学习个性化；在服务器端进行对比学习以对齐提取的特征，既不牺牲客户端个性化也不共享原始标签。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型家族上，相比现有最优方法，无/有OOD场景下测试准确率分别提升最高43.0%/28.3%，同时保持可接受的延迟。

Conclusion: HARMONY是首个支持异构客户端架构的混合拆分联邦学习框架，有效解决了表示偏斜问题，在准确率和延迟之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Mobile devices face diverse resource constraints and non-IID data class distributions, requiring fast on-device inference for local in-distribution (ID) classes and on-demand remote support for client-specific out-of-distribution (OOD) classes. Hybrid split federated learning (Hybrid SFL) couples personalized client-side front ends (supporting early exit) with a generalized server-side backend for fallback inference, balancing accuracy and cost. However, under client architectural heterogeneity, the existing hybrid SFL suffers from representation skew, where features from customized extractors fail to align in the shared space, leading to a sharp degradation in the server model responsible for OOD prediction. We propose HARMONY, the first hybrid SFL framework to support heterogeneous client architectures. HARMONY modifies meta-learning to simulate diverse extractors across parameters and architectures, and to learn to personalize. To mitigate representation skew, HARMONY conducts server-side contrastive learning to align extracted features, neither sacrificing clients' personalization nor sharing raw labels. Compared to the state of the art across multiple datasets and model families, HARMONY improves test accuracy by up to 43.0%/28.3% without/with OOD, respectively, while maintaining acceptable latency.

</details>


### [87] [Improved Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Smooth Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07218)
*Kun Long,Yuqiang Li,Xianyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对连续状态-动作空间的强化学习问题，提出了一种基于核平滑的模型方法，通过引入伯恩斯坦式探索奖励，在Lipschitz连续性假设下实现了比现有方法更优的遗憾界，特别是对时间步长依赖性的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典强化学习理论主要依赖低秩MDP的结构性假设，虽然提供了样本效率保证，但限制了其适用范围。核平滑方法利用MDP的平滑性作为替代范式，本文旨在进一步提升该类方法的理论性能。

Method: 在有限时域设定下，假设MDP满足Lipschitz连续性，采用非参数核平滑估计转移动态，并引入伯恩斯坦式探索奖励（Bernstein-style exploration bonus）来平衡探索与利用。此外，推导了一个新的紧伯恩斯坦型鞅浓度不等式作为理论工具。

Result: 所提出的方法在遗憾界上改进了现有最优结果，特别是在对时间步长（horizon）的依赖性方面取得了更优的阶数。

Conclusion: 伯恩斯坦式奖励与核平滑框架的协同作用能够有效提升在线强化学习在连续状态-动作空间中的理论保证，新鞅浓度不等式具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: For continuous state-action space scenarios, classical reinforcement learning (RL) theory predominantly focuses on low-rank Markov decision processes (MDPs), which provide sample-efficient guarantees at the expense of restrictive structural assumptions. Kernel smoothing model-based approaches offer a promising alternative paradigm that instead leverages the smoothness of the MDP and employs non-parametric kernel smoothing estimates of transition dynamics. This paper proposes a new kernel-smoothing model-based approach for online reinforcement learning in finite-horizon settings under Lipschitz continuity assumptions on the MDP. By incorporating a Bernstein-style exploration bonus into the kernel smoothing framework, our method achieves a regret bound which improves upon the state-of-the-art regret bound in its dependence on the horizon. The theoretical advancement relies on a delicate analysis of the synergy between Bernstein-style bonuses and kernel smoothing, where a new tight Bernstein-type concentration inequality for martingales may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [88] [On the Robustness of Distribution Support under Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07220)
*Ruijia Cao,Yuchen Wu,Nisha Chadramoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过建立“支持鲁棒性”性质，理论解释了扩散引导（Diffusion Guidance）为何能生成高质量样本——在精确得分函数条件下，引导扩散过程几乎总能生成靠近目标支持的样本，涵盖DDIM和DDPM及多种指数积分器离散化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散引导技术虽在实践中取得巨大成功，但其理论基础尚不清晰，缺乏对为何能持续产生高质量样本的理论解释。

Method: 通过建立“支持鲁棒性”（robustness of support）性质进行理论分析，证明在精确得分函数条件下，引导扩散过程生成的样本几乎总是保持在目标支持（support）附近。分析覆盖DDIM和DDPM两类模型，并适用于多种指数积分器诱导的离散化方案。

Result: 证明了引导扩散过程具有支持鲁棒性，即生成样本几乎总在目标支持附近，远离支持的样本在结构上通常不可靠且会影响下游任务。

Conclusion: 为扩散引导为何能生成物理上有意义且结构合理的样本提供了严格的理论基础，解释了其产生高质量样本的根本原因。

Abstract: Diffusion guidance is a powerful technique that enables controllable and high-fidelity sample generation with diffusion models. At a high level, it modifies the score function by incorporating a guidance term that steers the generative process toward a desired condition. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical properties of diffusion guidance remain largely unexplored, and it is not well understood why it consistently produces high-quality samples.
  In this work, we explain the effectiveness of diffusion guidance by establishing a \emph{robustness of support} property. Specifically, we show that, given exact access to the score functions, guided diffusion processes almost always generate samples that remain close to the target support. This property is particularly desirable, as samples that lie off the support are often structurally implausible and may adversely affect downstream tasks. Our analysis covers both Denoising Diffusion Implicit Models (DDIM) and Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPM), and applies to a wide range of discretization schemes induced by exponential integrators. Our results provide a rigorous foundation for understanding why diffusion guidance produces physically meaningful and structurally plausible samples.

</details>


### [89] [Don't Learn the Shape: Forecasting Periodic Time Series by Rank-1 Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07222)
*Takato Honda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高周期秩-1的周期性时间序列预测，简单的冻结基线（FLAIR）仅需28-57个标量参数即可匹配甚至超越复杂深度学习模型（如PatchTST），因为额外灵活性在此场景下仅是估计噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究周期性时间序列预测中究竟需要多少参数，验证在高度秩-1结构下（小时级电力数据重塑为24×N矩阵后近似秩-1），简单方法是否已足够。

Method: 将时间序列重塑为周期×周期数的矩阵，利用其近似秩-1结构；提出FLAIR方法：每个周期候选做一次SVD，GCV平均Ridge回归，无需GPU、预训练或逐任务调参。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval全部97个配置上，8种替代方法（Fourier, EWMA, James-Stein, rank-r SVD等）经Holm校正后无一显著优于冻结基线，两种显著更差。FLAIR在聚合GIFT-Eval上relMSE 0.838，匹配PatchTST的0.849。

Conclusion: 在高秩-1、多周期场景下，模型复杂度的增加主要引入估计噪声而非提升性能；FLAIR以极简参数实现高效、紧凑、快速的预测，挑战了"更复杂=更优"的假设。

Abstract: How few parameters do we really need to forecast a periodic time series? An hourly electricity series, reshaped as a 24-row matrix with one column per day, is approximately rank-1: a daily shape modulated by a daily level (median centered rank-1 energy 0.82 on GIFT-Eval). Should we learn the shape? Smoothing, shrinkage, and low-rank fits all seem like obvious upgrades over the simple average of the last K=2 cycles. On all 97 GIFT-Eval configurations, we tested 8 such alternatives (e.g., Fourier, EWMA, James-Stein, rank-r SVD): none significantly beats the frozen baseline under Holm correction; two are significantly worse. The resulting method, FLAIR, is (a) Effective: matches PatchTST on aggregate GIFT-Eval (relMASE 0.838 vs 0.849); (b) Compact: 28 scalars for hourly, 57 for weekly; (c) Fast: 22 minutes on one CPU core of a MacBook Pro; (d) Closed-form & Hands-Off: one SVD per period candidate, GCV-averaged Ridge, no GPU, no pre-training, no per-task tuning. In the high-rank-1, many-cycle regime, extra flexibility is estimation noise.

</details>


### [90] [Modulated learning for private and distributed regression with just a single sample per client device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07233)
*Praneeth Vepakomma,Amirhossein Reisizadeh,Samuel Horváth,Munther Dahleh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习中每个客户端仅有一个样本的场景，提出了一种通过注入单次校准噪声扰动并对数据进行后处理表示的方法，使设备能在保护隐私的前提下协作学习全局模型，其聚合后的无偏梯度与集中式非私有梯度期望一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习中，当每个客户端仅拥有一个样本时，标准的联邦学习范式会失效，因为基于单点的局部更新（尤其是在早期轮次）对模型系数的估计几乎没有帮助，而每轮添加的隐私噪声进一步削弱了这种效用。因此需要一种方法使这些设备能够在不泄露数据隐私的情况下有效协作学习全局模型。

Method: 每个客户端对其单一样本注入一次经过精心校准的噪声扰动，然后对扰动后的数据进行后处理表示，并将该表示共享给服务器。服务器聚合这些来自各客户端的表示，获得一个无偏梯度更新，该更新在期望上与未添加隐私保护的非私有集中式梯度相匹配。

Result: 该方法使每个客户端仅持有单个样本的设备能够协作学习到准确且保护隐私的模型，且聚合得到的梯度在期望上等价于非私有集中式梯度，实现了隐私保护与模型效用的兼顾。

Conclusion: 该方法不同于传统私有联邦学习（通信载荷为模型系数），而是传输经过隐私变换的数据样本表示，使得数据极有限的设备无需牺牲个体隐私即可协作学习有效的全局模型。

Abstract: This work focuses on the question of learning from a large number of devices with each device holding only a single sample of data. Several real-world applications exist to this one sample per client setup up including learning from fitness trackers, data/app usage aggregators, body-worn sensing devices, and daily event monitors to name a few. When a client has only one sample, the standard federated learning paradigm breaks down as a local update based on that single point is far from being useful, especially in the earlier rounds for estimation of the model coefficients. This utility is further weakened by the privacy-inducing noise applied at every round. This work caters to this problem to enable such clients to collaboratively contribute to effectively learn a global model without leaking the privacy of their data. The proposed approach injects a single, carefully calibrated noisy perturbation to transform the sample at each client, followed by a post-processed representation which is shared with the server. These representations aggregated at the server are processed to obtain an unbiased gradient update that in expectation matches the non-private centralized gradient while preserving data privacy. This approach is different than traditional private federated learning, where the communication payloads involve model coefficients as opposed to privately transformed data samples. This method enables devices with extremely limited data to collaborate and learn accurate, privacy-preserving models without requiring large local datasets or sacrificing individual privacy.

</details>


### [91] [Sample Complexity of Stochastic Optimization with Integer Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07239)
*Hongyu Cheng,Yinghao Zheng,Marco Molinaro,Amitabh Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了整数随机优化的样本复杂度，并与连续优化进行了比较，发现整数优化在某些情况下需要更多样本，在某些情况下需要更少样本，具体取决于目标和约束的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解整数随机优化相较于连续随机优化的样本复杂度差异，填补该领域理论分析的空白。

Method: 通过理论分析，分别在ℓ∞球子集上的Lipschitz目标、ℓ₂球子集上的Lipschitz目标以及强凸光滑目标三种设定下，推导并比较整数优化与连续优化的样本复杂度上下界。

Result: 1. ℓ∞球子集上，混合整数非线性非凸优化的统计复杂度与仅带边界约束的随机线性优化相同。2. ℓ₂球子集上，整数优化在某些情况下所需样本数严格小于连续优化。3. 强凸光滑目标下，整数优化需要Ω(1/ε²)样本，而连续优化仅需O(1/ε)。

Conclusion: 整数随机优化的样本复杂度与连续优化存在本质差异，且差异方向取决于问题结构，不能简单认为整数优化总是更难或更易。

Abstract: We establish sample complexity results for stochastic optimization over the integers, especially with a view to understand the complexity with respect to the corresponding continuous optimization problem. We show that integer optimization can sometimes require strictly more samples and sometimes strictly smaller number of samples, depending on the structure of the objective and constraints. 1. For Lipschitz objectives over subsets of the $\ell_\infty$ ball, the statistical complexity of general stochastic mixed-integer, nonlinear, nonconvex optimization is exactly the same as stochastic linear optimization with just bound constraints. 2. For Lipschitz objectives over subsets of the $\ell_2$ ball, we show that integer optimization can require strictly *smaller* sample size compared to the continuous setting in a certain regime. To get to this result, we also establish tight sample complexity results for nonconvex continuous stochastic optimization which, to the best of our knowledge, do not appear in prior work. 3. For strongly convex, smooth objectives, integer optimization has high statistical complexity compared to the continuous setting. In particular, we show that integer optimization requires $Ω(1/ε^2)$ samples to report an $ε$-approximate solution, compared to the well-known $O(1/ε)$ sample complexity from the continuous optimization literature.

</details>


### [92] [Experience Sharing in Mutual Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneous Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07244)
*Xiaoze Liu,Dhananjay Ram,Yuting Zhang,Zhaoyang Zhang,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出互强化学习框架，通过异构LLM策略间的类型化经验共享实现并发RL后训练，实验表明结果级共享在该框架下效果最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练方法中，不同模型族间的经验共享因参数、目标和分词器不兼容而受限，缺乏系统性的框架支持异构策略并发训练中的经验交换。

Method: 构建Mutual Reinforcement Learning框架，包含三大组件：共享经验交换(SEE)、多工作负载资源分配(MWRA)和分词器异构层(THL)。基于GRPO实例化三种探针：数据级rollout共享(PRP)、价值级优势共享(XGRPO)和结果级成功转移(SGT)。通过上下文赌博机分析刻画稳定-支持权衡。

Result: 结果级共享(SGT)在稳定性-支持权衡中占据最优位置；PRP需承担密度比方差和THL残差成本，XGRPO保持策略支持但改变标量基线，SGT向已验证的成功结果提供救援集分数方向。

Conclusion: 在评估范围内，结果级共享是异构LLM策略并发训练中经验共享的最优方式，平衡了稳定性与支持性需求。

Abstract: We introduce Mutual Reinforcement Learning, a framework for concurrent RL post-training in which heterogeneous LLM policies exchange typed experience while keeping separate parameters, objectives, and tokenizers. The framework combines a Shared Experience Exchange (SEE), Multi-Worker Resource Allocation (MWRA), and a Tokenizer Heterogeneity Layer (THL) that retokenizes text and aligns token-level traces across incompatible vocabularies. This substrate makes the experience-sharing design question operational across model families. We instantiate three controlled probes on top of GRPO: data-level rollout sharing via Peer Rollout Pooling (PRP), value-level advantage sharing via Cross-Policy GRPO Advantage Sharing (XGRPO), and outcome-level success transfer via Success-Gated Transfer (SGT). A contextual-bandit analysis characterizes their structural positions on a stability-support trade-off: PRP pays density-ratio variance and THL residual costs, XGRPO preserves on-policy actor support while changing scalar baselines, and SGT supplies a rescue-set score direction toward verified peer successes. In the evaluated regime, outcome-level sharing occupies the favorable point of this trade-off.

</details>


### [93] [When Are Experts Misrouted? Counterfactual Routing Analysis in Mixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07260)
*Youngsik Yoon,Siwei Wang,Wei Chen,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究MoE语言模型的路由选择质量，发现标准top-k路由器在推理困难token上存在路由分配不当问题，且仅更新最终层路由器的简单方法即可提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型的路由训练仅通过语言建模损失评估被选中的路由，而未被选中的替代路由的效用从未被直接评估，导致难以判断当前路由选择是否最优。

Method: 固定模型参数，对每个token的当前路由与等计算量的采样替代路由进行比较，以路由赋予实际token的next-token概率作为效用分数；进一步对最终层路由器进行仅路由器的微调（不更新专家和其他层路由器）。

Result: 标准路由器在高置信度token上路由与效用对齐良好，但在驱动困难推理的脆弱token上存在大量等计算量但更低损失的替代路由未被选中；该模式在Qwen3-30B-A3B、GPT-OSS-20B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite、OLMoE-1B-7B四个模型上一致成立；仅更新最终层路由器即可在AIME 2024+2025和HMMT 2025上提升pass@K。

Conclusion: MoE路由失败的部分原因源于路由器本身可达的分配不当（而非专家容量限制），标准top-k路由训练目标（仅评估执行路由的损失+负载均衡聚合统计）的结构性缺陷是根本原因。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models route each token to a small subset of experts, but whether the routes selected by a trained top-$k$ router are good ones is rarely evaluated directly. Holding the model fixed, we compare each standard route against sampled equal-compute alternatives for the same token and score each by the next-token probability it assigns to the realized token in a verified reasoning trajectory. The result is sharply token-conditional: the standard router is well-aligned with route utility on confident tokens but uninformative on the fragile tokens that drive hard reasoning, where lower-loss equal-compute routes consistently exist inside the frozen model but are not selected. The same pattern holds across Qwen3-30B-A3B, GPT-OSS-20B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and OLMoE-1B-7B, and follows structurally from how standard top-$k$ training evaluates routing decisions: the language modeling loss scores only the executed route, and load balancing depends only on aggregate routing statistics. A minimal router-only update to the final-layer router, leaving every expert and every other router frozen, is sufficient to shift pass@K on AIME 2024+2025 and HMMT 2025 for both Qwen3-30B-A3B and GPT-OSS-20B, suggesting that at least part of the failure reflects router-reachable misallocation rather than expert capacity alone.

</details>


### [94] [PerCaM-Health: Personalized Dynamic Causal Graphs for Healthcare Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07267)
*Elahe Khatibi,Ziyu Wang,Saba A. Farahani,Di Huang,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PerCaM-Health是一种从纵向健康数据中学习个性化动态因果图的框架，通过知识引导的群体图与个体时序证据的保守自适应演化，支持患者级别的反事实干预推理，在半合成健康基准上优于群体级和个体级基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序因果发现方法在个性化医疗决策中存在群体模型不个性化、个体发现不可靠的根本性 gap，无法处理个体轨迹短、噪声大、非平稳等挑战。

Method: 先学习知识引导的群体时序图，再通过患者特异性时序证据和滚动窗口更新进行保守自适应演化，生成可解释的图序列，并与时序结构方程结合支持反事实查询。

Result: 在半合成动态健康基准上，PerCaM-Health在图恢复、动态边追踪和干预方向准确性上均优于群体级、个体级和非个性化基线方法。

Conclusion: 联合建模个性化和时序演化能够产生更可靠的因果结构及干预推理能力。

Abstract: Personalized healthcare decisions require reasoning about how physiological and behavioral variables influence an individual patient over time. Existing temporal causal discovery methods are poorly matched to this setting: cohort-level models provide stable but non-personalized structures, while per-patient discovery is unreliable because individual trajectories are short, noisy, irregular, and non-stationary. This creates a fundamental gap between population-level causal modeling and the patient-specific, time-varying mechanisms needed for intervention reasoning. We introduce PerCaM-Health, a framework for learning personalized dynamic causal graphs from longitudinal health data. The framework learns a knowledge-guided population temporal graph, then conservatively adapts and evolves it using patient-specific temporal evidence and rolling-window updates, producing interpretable and auditable graph sequences. By coupling these graphs with temporal structural equations, the framework enables patient-level counterfactual queries, such as estimating short-horizon outcome changes under hypothetical behavioral interventions. Experiments on a semi-synthetic dynamic health benchmark show that PerCaM-Health improves graph recovery, dynamic edge tracking, and intervention direction accuracy compared to cohort-level, per-patient, and non-personalized temporal baselines. These results demonstrate that jointly modeling personalization and temporal evolution yields more reliable causal structure and intervention reasoning.

</details>


### [95] [Bifurcation Models: Learning Set-Valued Solution Maps with Weight-Tied Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07277)
*Caleb Jore,Jialin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分岔模型（bifurcation models），通过权重共享的动力学视角，使不同初始化收敛到不同稳定平衡点，从而表示吸引子景观而非单一分支。证明宽泛集值映射可由正则平衡动力学表示，且诱导选择子几乎处处正则，而人工选择子可任意不规则。在受挫Ising模型和Allen-Cahn方程上的实验展示了该方法无需分支标签即可发现多个有效平衡点，并揭示了准确率与多样性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学和组合问题存在多个正确解，而标准监督学习通过选择单一解作为目标来消除歧义，但这一隐式选择器可能是任意、不连续的，且比底层解集更难学习。作者旨在通过动力学视角避免人工选择器带来的问题，使模型自然地表示整个解空间。

Method: 采用分岔模型（bifurcation models），即权重共享的动力学系统，不同初始化可收敛到不同稳定平衡点，从而将模型表示为吸引子景观而非单一分支。从理论上证明了宽泛集值映射（具有局部Lipschitz分支）可由正则平衡动力学表示，并分析了诱导选择子的正则性。实验采用受挫Ising模型和Allen-Cahn方程进行验证。

Result: 理论证明：具有局部Lipschitz分支的集值映射可用正则平衡动力学表示，且诱导选择子几乎处处正则，而人工选择子可任意不规则。实验：在受挫Ising模型上，动力学方法无需分支标签即可发现多个有效平衡点，且优于单分支监督学习；在Allen-Cahn实验上，多样性不会自动出现，需要显式鼓励，但存在准确率-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 分岔模型通过动力学视角有效处理多解问题，避免了人工选择器的缺陷，但多样性的获取需要显式鼓励且存在权衡。

Abstract: Many scientific and combinatorial problems admit multiple correct solutions, not a single label. Standard supervised learning resolves this ambiguity by choosing one solution as the target, but this hidden selector can be arbitrary, discontinuous, and harder to learn than the underlying solution set. We study bifurcation models, a weight-tied dynamical view in which different initializations can converge to different stable equilibria, so the model represents an attractor landscape rather than one chosen branch. We prove that broad set-valued maps with locally Lipschitz branches can be represented by regular equilibrium dynamics and that the induced selectors are almost everywhere regular, while manual selectors can be arbitrarily irregular. Experiments on frustrated Ising models show that such dynamics can discover multiple valid equilibria without branch labels and outperform single-branch supervision. Allen--Cahn experiments further show that diversity is not automatic: it can be encouraged explicitly, but with an accuracy--diversity tradeoff.

</details>


### [96] [Predictive but Not Plannable: RC-aux for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07278)
*Wenyuan Li,Guang Li,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RC-aux辅助目标，通过多时域开环预测和预算约束可达性监督，修正隐空间世界模型在短时预测与长程规划之间的时空不匹配问题，在保持模型主干不变的前提下提升规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐空间世界模型常以局部预测目标训练，导致隐空间在长程目标导向搜索中与规划对齐不良，欧氏距离无法反映有限动作预算下的实际可达性，存在时空不匹配问题。

Method: RC-aux沿时间轴引入多时域开环预测训练，沿空间轴引入预算约束的可达性监督与时序硬负样本，使隐空间区分最终可达与当前规划时域内可达的状态；测试时可达性信号可被规划器用于偏好既目标导向又在预算内可达的轨迹。

Result: 在LeWorldModel上实例化RC-aux，在目标条件像素控制任务和LIBERO-Goal扩展上，以较低额外成本提升了LeWM风格的规划性能。

Conclusion: 隐世界模型的规划性能不仅取决于预测精度，还取决于学习到的表示是否编码了下游搜索所需的时间与几何结构。

Abstract: A latent world model may achieve accurate short-horizon prediction while still inducing a latent space that is poorly aligned with planning. A key issue is spatiotemporal mismatch: these models are often trained with local predictive supervision, but deployed for long-horizon goal-directed search in latent spaces where Euclidean distance may not reflect what is reachable within a finite action budget. We present the Reachability-Correction auxiliary objective (RC-aux), a lightweight correction for this mismatch in reconstruction-free latent world models. RC-aux keeps the world-model backbone unchanged and adds planning-aligned supervision along two axes. Along the time axis, multi-horizon open-loop prediction trains the model beyond one-step consistency. Along the space axis, budget-conditioned reachability supervision, together with temporal hard negatives, encourages the latent space to distinguish states that are eventually reachable from those reachable within the current planning horizon. At test time, the learned reachability signal can also be used by a reachability-aware planner to favor trajectories that are both goal-directed and attainable under the available budget. We instantiate RC-aux on LeWorldModel and evaluate it under both continuation-training and matched-from-scratch settings. Across goal-conditioned pixel-control tasks and a LIBERO-Goal extension, RC-aux improves LeWM-style planning with modest additional cost. These results suggest that planning with latent world models depends not only on predictive accuracy, but also on whether the learned representation encodes the temporal and geometric structure required by downstream search. The code is available at https://github.com/Guang000/RC-aux.

</details>


### [97] [Mask2Cause: Causal Discovery via Adjacency Constrained Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07280)
*Omar Muhammad,Pasupuleti Dhruv Shivkant,Deepak N. Subramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mask2Cause是一个端到端的因果发现框架，通过逆向变量嵌入和邻域约束掩码注意力机制，在时间序列预测前向传播过程中直接恢复因果图，在多个基准上实现了最先进的因果发现性能，同时将预测模型的参数量平均减少70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度时间序列因果发现方法存在两个问题：一是基于组件式架构无法捕捉共享系统动态，二是使用解耦的事后图提取容易过拟合到虚假相关。因此需要一种端到端的、能在学习过程中直接恢复因果结构的方法。

Method: 提出Mask2Cause框架，核心包括：(1) 逆向变量嵌入(Inverted Variable Embedding)；(2) 邻域约束掩码注意力(Adjacency-Constrained Masked Attention)机制；(3) 使用同方差或异方差目标函数进行训练，以同时捕捉均值和方差中的因果影响。整个框架在预测前向传播过程中端到端地恢复因果图。

Result: 在多种基准上（从合成混沌动力学到现实生物模拟）实现了最先进的因果发现性能，参数量显著低于标准基线。使用推断出的因果结构可将预测模型参数量平均减少70%以上，同时保持预测精度。

Conclusion: Mask2Cause证明了端到端方式在时间序列因果发现中的有效性，不仅能更准确地发现因果关系，还能利用发现的因果结构大幅压缩预测模型，实现高效且准确的时序建模。

Abstract: Leveraging deep learning for causal discovery in time series remains challenging because existing neural methods predominantly rely on component-wise architectures that fail to capture shared system dynamics or employ decoupled post-hoc graph extraction that risks overfitting to spurious correlations. We propose $\textbf{Mask2Cause}$, an end-to-end framework that recovers the underlying causal graph directly during the forecasting forward pass. Our approach introduces an Inverted Variable Embedding and an Adjacency-Constrained Masked Attention mechanism, trained with homoscedastic or heteroscedastic objectives to capture causal influences in both mean and variance. Empirical results on diverse benchmarks, from synthetic chaotic dynamics to realistic biological simulations, demonstrate state-of-the-art causal discovery with significantly reduced parameter complexity compared to standard baselines. We further show that inferred causal structures can be used to reduce parameter count of forecasting models by more than 70% on average while maintaining predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [98] [The Convergence Gap: Instruction-Tuned Language Models Stabilize Later in the Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07282)
*Yifan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“收敛差距”诊断方法，发现指令微调模型比预训练模型更晚收敛到最终预测分布，且晚期MLP层是控制该延迟的最强双向操作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注模型最终输出质量，但忽略了模型内部各层逐token预测分布的动态收敛过程，尤其是指令微调如何改变这一过程尚不明确。

Method: 提出收敛差距（convergence gap）诊断方法，解码每层的next-token分布并计算与最终分布的KL散度；在6对预训练和指令微调模型上比较；通过端点匹配的原始/调优读出、无端点同历史检查、固定历史模板重放等对照组验证；使用匹配前缀干预（matched-prefix interventions）定位关键层。

Result: 指令微调模型在堆栈后期仍偏离最终预测分布；晚期MLP移植（IT→PT）使后期KL增加+0.34 nats，PT晚期移植到IT减少-0.51 nats；随机扰动仅+0.003 vs 真实移植+0.327；Gemma案例研究支持该现象的行为合理性。

Conclusion: 指令微调模型表现出“更晚收敛”的预测动态特征，晚期MLP计算是控制该延迟的最强测试双向操作点，为理解后训练如何改变内部计算提供了新视角。

Abstract: Final outputs hide when a checkpoint commits to its next-token prediction. We introduce the convergence gap, a model-diffing diagnostic that decodes each layer's next-token distribution and measures its distance to the model's own final distribution. Across six paired pretrained and instruction-tuned checkpoints in native prompting regimes, instruction-tuned checkpoints remain farther from their final predictions later into the stack. The effect persists under endpoint-matched raw and tuned readouts, endpoint-free same-history checks, and fixed-history template replay. Matched-prefix interventions identify late MLP windows as the largest tested leverage point: late IT grafts into PT hosts increase late KL by +0.34 nats, while PT-late swaps into IT hosts reduce it by -0.51 nats; matched random late perturbations give only +0.003 versus +0.327 for the true late graft. A preselected Gemma case study provides behavior-facing plausibility for the same late swap, without serving as a benchmark claim. These results identify a robust predictiondynamics signature of post-training: released instruction-following checkpoints tend to settle later, and late MLP computation is the strongest tested bidirectional handle on that delay under matched histories.

</details>


### [99] [Pretraining Induces a Reusable Spectral Basis for Downstream Task Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07302)
*Junjie Yu,Yue Wang,Zihan Deng,Yan Zhu,Wenxiao Ma,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现预训练模型微调时，预训练权重矩阵的主奇异向量保持高度稳定，且跨任务共享。基于此，提出仅优化主奇异系数的方法，在GLUE上用0.2%可训练参数取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注微调发生在低维子空间，但忽略了未探索方向的性质：它们是无关噪声还是已编码任务相关结构？理解这一点对揭示预训练知识如何迁移至关重要。

Method: 通过系统谱分析（视觉和语言模型），研究预训练权重矩阵奇异向量在微调中的稳定性；提出冻结预训练奇异向量、仅优化主奇异系数的参数高效微调方法。

Result: 预训练权重矩阵的主奇异向量在微调中高度稳定，且跨无关下游任务共享；更大规模预训练数据带来更高的谱稳定性；所提方法在GLUE上用0.2%可训练参数达到竞争性能。

Conclusion: 稳定方向编码的是可迁移的结构信息而非无关噪声；成功的预训练发现了下游任务继承并运作其中的谱基座。

Abstract: Finetuning pretrained models occurs in a low-dimensional subspace of the full parameter space. Prior work has focused on characterizing this optimization subspace, but largely ignored the complementary question: why do certain directions remain unexplored during finetuning? Are these stable directions irrelevant to downstream tasks, or do they already encode task-relevant structure that requires no further adjustment? Answering this question is central to understanding how pretrained knowledge transfers. Through systematic spectral analysis across vision and language models, we show that the leading singular vectors of pretrained weight matrices remain highly stable under finetuning and are shared across unrelated downstream tasks, revealing that pretraining establishes a reusable spectral coordinate system. Models pretrained on larger datasets exhibit greater spectral stability under distribution shift or task change, directly linking pretraining scale to geometric transferability. Motivated by these findings, we propose a parameter-efficient method that freezes pretrained singular vectors and optimizes only leading spectral coefficients, achieving competitive performance on GLUE with 0.2% trainable parameters. Our results reveal that the stable directions encode transferable structure rather than irrelevant noise: successful pretraining discovers spectral bases that downstream tasks inherit and operate within.

</details>


### [100] [Latent Order Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07304)
*Emil Carlsson,Newton Mwai,Fredrik D. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在序赌博机（LOB），放宽了传统潜在赌博机对离散潜在状态和奖励分布精确建模的要求，仅需知道每个状态下动作偏好的部分序先验知识，允许同一状态的不同实例在奖励尺度上存在差异，并给出了相应的上置信界算法和后验采样算法及理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统潜在赌博机算法要求已知且准确的离散潜在状态和奖励分布后验，但实际中获取精确结构模型困难，且有限潜在状态可能不足以刻画所有问题实例的奖励分布，导致样本效率低。

Method: 提出潜在序赌博机（LOB）框架，仅需每个状态下动作偏好的部分序先验知识；针对完全序和部分序两种情况，设计了上置信界（UCB）算法并给出遗憾上界；为提升实证性能，还提出后验采样算法。

Result: 在实验中，所提算法在共享奖励参数时与全先验潜在赌博机竞争力相当，在奖励尺度不同的情况下优于传统方法。

Conclusion: LOB通过放宽对精确奖励分布的假设，仅利用动作偏好的部分序结构，有效减少了样本需求，提高了在奖励分布异质场景下的适用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Bandit algorithms solve diverse sequential decision-making problems, but are often too sample-inefficient for from-scratch personalization. To substantially reduce exploration times, latent bandit algorithms exploit cross-instance structure implied by discrete latent states, provided that the posterior distribution of rewards and latent states is known and accurate. However, obtaining an accurate model of this structure is difficult, and a small number of latent states may be insufficient to characterize the reward distributions in all problem instances. We propose latent order bandits (LOB), relaxing the assumptions of latent bandits to require only prior knowledge of a partial order of action preferences in each state. This allows instances of the same state to vary in reward distributions, as long as the partial order of actions is shared. For example, groups of users on a streaming service may agree on which movie genres are the best but rate experiences on different scales. We give an upper-confidence bound procedure for the LOB problem, applicable to both total and partial latent orders, and give an upper bound on its regret. To improve empirical performance, we propose a posterior-sampling algorithm and show, in a suite of experiments, that both are competitive with full-prior latent bandits when same-state instances share reward parameters, and preferable to them when reward scales differ between instances with the same latent state.

</details>


### [101] [Generative Modeling with Flux Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07319)
*Peter Pao-Huang,Xiaojie Qiu,Stefano Ermon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flux Matching 是一种新的生成建模范式，通过放宽传统基于分数的模型对保守向量场的限制，允许存在无限多个以数据为平稳分布的向量场，从而将向量场从固定目标转变为可设计的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于分数的生成模型要求模型等于数据分数（即保守向量场），这种强约束限制了模型的灵活性。作者希望提出一种更宽松的条件，使得向量场本身可以成为设计选择，从而引入归纳偏置、结构先验和动力学特性。

Method: 提出 Flux Matching 目标函数，该目标施加的条件比分数匹配更弱，允许学习无限多个向量场，只要其平稳分布等于数据分布。通过优化该目标，模型不再受限于保守向量场，从而能够学习传统分数匹配无法学习的生成模型。

Result: 在高维图像数据集上表现强劲，并且解锁了多项应用：更快的采样、可解释的机制模型、以及编码变量间有向依赖关系的动力学。

Conclusion: Flux Matching 通过将向量场从固定目标转变为设计参数，为生成建模开辟了新的维度，提供了超越传统分数匹配的灵活性和应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Flux Matching, a new paradigm for generative modeling that generalizes existing score-based models to a broader family of vector fields that need not be conservative. Rather than requiring the model to equal the data score, the Flux Matching objective imposes a weaker condition that admits infinitely many vector fields whose stationary distribution is the data. This flexibility enables a class of generative models that cannot be learned under score matching, in which inductive biases, structural priors, and properties of the dynamics can be directly imposed or optimized. We show that Flux Matching performs strongly on high-dimensional image datasets and, more importantly, that our added freedom unlocks a range of applications including faster sampling, interpretable and mechanistic models, and dynamics that encode directed dependencies between variables. More broadly, Flux Matching opens a new dimension in generative modeling by turning the vector field itself into a design choice rather than a fixed target. Code is available at https://github.com/peterpaohuang/flux_matching.

</details>


### [102] [Rethinking Importance Sampling in LLM Policy Optimization: A Cumulative Token Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07331)
*Yuheng Zhang,Chenlu Ye,Shuowei Jin,Changlong Yu,Wei Xiong,Saurabh Sahu,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CTPO方法，使用累积token重要性采样比（Cumulative Token IS Ratio）解决强化学习策略梯度估计中的偏差-方差困境，在数学推理任务上取得优于GRPO和GSPO的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（PPO、GRPO、GSPO）在off-policy策略梯度估计中面临偏差-方差困境：token级IS比率引入前缀分布不匹配的偏差，完整序列比率因token比率的乘积累积导致高方差，GSPO通过长度归一化改善数值稳定性但偏离了精确的完整序列IS校正。需要一种兼具无偏性和低方差的IS比率设计。

Method: 1) 提出累积token IS比率（从开始到位置t的per-token比率乘积），理论上证明其对每个token级梯度项提供无偏的前缀校正，且方差严格低于完整序列比率。2) 提出CTPO（Cumulative Token Policy Optimization），结合累积token IS比率与位置自适应裁剪（Position-Adaptive Clipping），在log空间中根据累积log比率的自然√t增长来缩放裁剪边界，实现跨token位置的一致性正则化。

Result: 在工具集成推理场景的多个数学推理基准上，CTPO在两个模型规模下均取得了优于GRPO和GSPO基线的最佳平均表现。

Conclusion: 累积token IS比率在理论上兼具无偏性与低方差优势，CTPO通过位置自适应裁剪进一步提升了训练稳定性，实验验证了该方法在数学推理任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning, including reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), has emerged as a powerful approach for LLM post-training. Central to these approaches is the design of the importance sampling (IS) ratio used in off-policy policy-gradient estimation. Existing methods face a fundamental bias-variance dilemma: token-level IS ratios, as adopted by PPO (Schulman et al., 2017) and GRPO (Shao et al., 2024), introduce bias by ignoring prefix state distribution mismatch; full sequence ratios provide exact trajectory-level correction but suffer from high variance due to the multiplicative accumulation of per-token ratios, while GSPO (Zheng et al., 2025) improves numerical stability via length normalization at the cost of deviating from the exact full-sequence IS correction. In this work, we identify the cumulative token IS ratio, the product of per-token ratios up to position $t$, as a theoretically principled solution to this dilemma. We prove that, under the token-level policy-gradient formulation, this ratio provides an unbiased prefix correction for each token-level gradient term and has strictly lower variance than the full sequence ratio. Building on this insight, we propose CTPO (Cumulative Token Policy Optimization), which combines the cumulative token IS ratio with position-adaptive clipping that scales log-space clip bounds according to the natural $\sqrt{t}$ growth of the cumulative log-ratio. This yields more consistent regularization across token positions. We implement and evaluate CTPO in the tool-integrated reasoning setting on several challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, achieving the best average performance across both model scales compared with strong GRPO and GSPO baselines. Code will be available at https://github.com/horizon-llm/CTPO.

</details>


### [103] [Beyond Linear Attention: Softmax Transformers Implement In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07333)
*Zixuan Xie,Xinyu Liu,Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Rohan Chandra,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在理论上分析了使用标准softmax注意力的Transformer如何实现上下文强化学习，证明其前向传播等价于加权softmax时序差分学习算法的迭代更新，并建立了参数收敛性和全局最优性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有ICRL理论分析大多依赖线性注意力（用恒等映射替换softmax）这一不现实的简化假设，缺乏对实际使用的标准softmax注意力机制的理论理解。

Method: 论文将softmax注意力Transformer的前向传播解释为加权softmax TD学习算法的迭代更新；通过收缩条件分析策略评估误差随层数增加的衰减；并通过预训练损失证明所识别参数是全局极小值点。

Result: 发现softmax注意力Transformer的前向传播等价于加权softmax TD迭代；在一定收缩条件下策略评估误差随层数增加而衰减；所识别的参数是预训练损失的全局极小值。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在不依赖线性注意力简化假设的情况下，为softmax注意力Transformer的ICRL行为提供了严格的理论基础，揭示了新提出的加权softmax TD算法统一了线性TD和表格TD两种特例。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) studies agents that, after pretraining, adapt to new tasks by conditioning on additional context without parameter updates. Existing theoretical analyses of ICRL largely rely on linear attention, which replaces the softmax function in the standard attention with an identity mapping. This paper provides the first theoretical understanding of ICRL without making the unrealistic linear attention simplification. In particular, we consider the standard softmax attention used in practice. We show that, with certain parameters, the layerwise forward pass of a Transformer with such softmax attention is equivalent to iterative updates of a weighted softmax temporal difference (TD) learning algorithm. Here, weighted softmax TD is a new RL algorithm that performs policy evaluation in kernel space and adopts both linear TD and tabular TD as special cases. We also prove that under a certain contraction condition, the policy evaluation error decays as the number of layers grows, with the identified parameters above. Finally, we prove that those parameters are a global minimizer of a pretraining loss, explaining their emergence in our numerical experiments.

</details>


### [104] [CellScientist: Dual-Space Hierarchical Orchestration for Closed-Loop Refinement of Virtual Cell Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07335)
*Mengran Li,Bo Li,Jiaying Wang,Wenbin Xing,Yixuan Dong,Chengyang Zhang,Hongliang Zhang,Yuzhong Peng,Jinlin Wu,Bob Zhang,Bingo Wing-Kuen Ling,Fuji Yang,Zhen Lei,Jiebo Luo,Zelin Zang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CellScientist提出了一种双空间层次框架，通过假设空间与实现空间的耦合，将虚拟细胞建模中的执行失败转化为结构化信号进行定向修正，实现了从"调试"到"模型精化"的闭环迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM辅助建模工作流存在精化-路由问题：预测偏差通过可执行实现被观测到，但相关修正可能涉及建模假设、表征设计、实现或任务约束等多个层面，缺乏结构化的反馈传播机制，导致迭代精化可能只修复代码而未能修正根本假设。

Method: 提出双空间层次框架CellScientist，包含高层假设空间（结构化建模决策状态）和低层可执行实现空间（符合任务与接口约束的合法程序），将执行偏差路由回对应的假设或实现更新，形成假设→实现→假设的闭环。

Result: 在形态学与转录组学基准测试以及单细胞扰动评估中，CellScientist选择的最终可执行模型在固定划分与评估协议下优于参考基线，且工作流产生可审计的精化轨迹。

Conclusion: 通过将建模失败转化为结构化信号而非调试事件，CellScientist有效解决了LLM辅助建模中的精化-路由问题，实现了跨层次的定向模型迭代精化。

Abstract: Virtual Cell Modeling (VCM) requires models that not only predict perturbation responses, but also support targeted revision when predictions fail. Current LLM-assisted modeling workflows face a refinement-routing problem: prediction discrepancies are observed through executable implementations, but the relevant revision may involve the modeling assumption, representation design, implementation, or task constraint. Without structured feedback propagation across these levels, iterative refinement may repair code while failing to revise the assumption responsible for the discrepancy. We propose CellScientist, a dual-space hierarchical framework that couples a high-level hypothesis space with a low-level executable implementation space. CellScientist represents modeling decisions as structured states, realizes them as admissible programs under task and interface constraints, and routes execution discrepancies back to targeted hypothesis or implementation updates. This enables a closed Hypothesis -> Implementation -> Hypothesis loop where failures become structured signals for model refinement rather than debugging events. Across morphology and transcriptomic benchmarks, with additional single-cell perturbation evaluations, the final executable models selected by CellScientist improve over reference baselines under fixed split and evaluation protocols, while the workflow produces auditable refinement traces.

</details>


### [105] [Mage: Multi-Axis Evaluation of LLM-Generated Executable Game Scenes Beyond Compile-Pass Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07342)
*Hugh Xuechen Liu,Kıvanç Tatar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在可执行游戏场景合成领域，编译通过率与功能正确性呈负相关，多轴评估框架Mage揭示了这一现象，其中直接NL-to-C#生成虽运行时通过率最高（43%），但结构空洞（机制F1≈0.12）；结构IR条件化虽降低运行时通过率，但恢复了领域忠实结构（F1可达1.00）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成以编译通过率作为主要评估指标，但对于多组件领域特定工件，该指标具有误导性。作者旨在证明编译通过率与功能正确性之间的背离，并提出多轴评估的必要性。

Method: 提出四轴评估协议Mage（编译成功、运行时成功、结构保真度、机制遵循性），对4个开源LLM（7B-30B）的858次生成尝试、26个手编Unity目标模式可玩概念及两种自动提取的IR粒度级别进行评估。

Result: 直接NL-to-C#生成运行时通过率最高（均值43%），但机制F1仅约0.12；结构IR条件化将运行时通过率减半，但F1可达1.00；行为仅与全场景粒度在统计上无显著差异（McNemar p=1.0），表明输入级粒度饱和。

Conclusion: 编译通过率在该领域与功能正确性呈反相关，多轴评估是检测这种背离的必要手段。发布了基准测试、回放日志和逐记录指标以供独立验证。

Abstract: Compile-pass rate is the dominant evaluation signal for LLM code generation, yet for multi-component domain-specific artifacts it can be actively misleading. We demonstrate this on executable game scene synthesis with a four-axis evaluation protocol (named `Mage') -- compile success, runtime success, structural fidelity, and mechanism adherence -- applied to 858 generation attempts across four open-weight LLMs (7B--30B), 26~hand-crafted Unity goal pattern playable concepts, and two automatically extracted IR granularity levels. Direct NL-to-C\# generation achieves the highest runtime-pass rate (43\% mean) yet produces structurally vacuous scenes (mechanism $F_1 \approx 0.12$). Structural IR conditioning halves the runtime rate but recovers domain-faithful structure ($F_1$ up to 1.00). Within IR conditioning, behavior-only and full-scene granularity are statistically indistinguishable (McNemar $p = 1.0$), indicating input-level granularity saturation. These results show that compile rate is anti-correlated with functional correctness in this domain and that multi-axis evaluation is necessary to detect the divergence. We release the benchmark, replay logs, and per-record metrics for independent verification.

</details>


### [106] [MISA: Mixture of Indexer Sparse Attention for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07363)
*Ruijie Zhou,Fanxu Meng,Yufei Xu,Tongxuan Liu,Guangming Lu,Muhan Zhang,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MISA提出将DSA索引器的多头设计替换为混合专家（MoE）架构，通过轻量级路由器选择少量活跃头执行令牌级评分，在保持性能的同时大幅降低推理成本，并实现约3.82倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA的索引器使用大量查询头（如64头）共享同一选定令牌集，这种多头设计在长上下文场景下成为主导计算成本，亟需降低其计算开销。

Method: 将DSA索引器的多头视为MoE专家池，通过轻量级路由器基于块级统计信息选择少量查询相关头，仅这些头执行高成本的令牌级评分；进一步提出层次化变体，先用路由结果扩大候选集，再用原始DSA索引器重排序以精确恢复最终选择。

Result: 仅用8个活跃头、无需额外训练，在LongBench上匹配DSA索引器性能，索引器头数减少8倍（DeepSeek-V3.2）和4倍（GLM-5），平均优于HISA；128K上下文下保持完全绿色的Needle-in-a-Haystack热力图，每层恢复DSA索引器92%以上选中令牌；TileLang内核实现约3.82倍加速。

Conclusion: MISA作为DSA索引器的即插即用替代方案，通过MoE路由显著降低计算成本而不牺牲性能，为细粒度稀疏注意力提供了更高效的实践路径。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) sets the state of the art for fine-grained inference-time sparse attention by introducing a learned token-wise indexer that scores every prefix token and selects the most relevant ones for the main attention. To remain expressive, the indexer uses many query heads (for example, 64 on DeepSeek-V3.2) that share the same selected token set; this multi-head design is precisely what makes the indexer the dominant cost on long contexts. We propose MISA (Mixture of Indexer Sparse Attention), a drop-in replacement for the DSA indexer that treats its indexer heads as a pool of mixture-of-experts. A lightweight router uses cheap block-level statistics to pick a query-dependent subset of only a few active heads, and only those heads run the heavy token-level scoring. This preserves the diversity of the original indexer pool while reducing the per-query cost from scoring every prefix token with every head to scoring it with only a handful of routed heads, plus a negligible router term computed on a small set of pooled keys. We further introduce a hierarchical variant of MISA that uses the routed pass to keep an enlarged candidate set and then re-ranks it with the original DSA indexer to recover the final selected tokens almost exactly. With only eight active heads and no additional training, MISA matches the dense DSA indexer on LongBench across DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5 while running with eight and four times fewer indexer heads respectively, and outperforms HISA on average. It also preserves fully green Needle-in-a-Haystack heatmaps up to a 128K-token context and recovers more than 92% of the tokens selected by the DSA indexer per layer. Our TileLang kernel delivers roughly a 3.82 times speedup over DSA's original indexer kernel on a single NVIDIA H200 GPU.

</details>


### [107] [StreamPhy: Streaming Inference of High-Dimensional Physical Dynamics via State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07384)
*Panqi Chen,Yifan Sun,Shikai Fang,Xiao Fu,Lei Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StreamPhy是一个端到端框架，能在实时流式场景下从不规则稀疏测量中高效、准确地推断高维多模态物理场动力学，融合了自适应观测编码器、结构化状态空间模型和FT-FiLM解码器，相比现有方法在精度上提升至少48%，推理速度提升20-100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散生成模型和函数张量方法通常只能在离线设置下运行，依赖完整时间观测数据，且推理成本高昂，无法满足从实时不规则稀疏测量中推断高维多模态物理场演化的需求。

Method: StreamPhy框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 数据自适应观测编码器，对任意观测模式具有鲁棒性；(2) 结构化状态空间模型，支持跨不规则时间间隔的存储高效在线更新；(3) 函数张量特征线性调制（FT-FiLM）解码器，用于连续场生成。论文证明了FT-FiLM比函数Tucker模型更具表达能力。

Result: 在三个代表性物理系统上的实验表明，StreamPhy在挑战性采样模式下持续优于现有基线方法，精度提升至少48%，推理速度相比扩散方法提升20-100倍。

Conclusion: StreamPhy为高维物理场的实时流式推断提供了一种高效、准确的端到端解决方案，其FT-FiLM模块的理论表达能力优势得到了证明。

Abstract: Inferring the evolution of high-dimensional and multi-modal (e.g., spatio-temporal) physical fields from irregular sparse measurements in real time is a fundamental challenge in science and engineering. Existing approaches, including diffusion-based generative models and functional tensor methods, typically operate in offline settings, depend on full temporal observations, or incur substantial inference cost. We propose StreamPhy, an end-to-end framework that enables efficient and accurate streaming inference of full-field physical dynamics from incoming irregular sparse measurements. The framework integrates a data-adaptive observation encoder that is robust to arbitrary observation patterns, a structured state-space model that supports memory-efficient online updates across irregular time intervals, and an expressive Functional Tensor Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FT-FiLM) decoder for continuous-field generation. We prove that FT-FiLM is more expressive than the functional Tucker model, admitting a richer function class for handling complex dynamics. Experiments on three representative physical systems under challenging sampling patterns show that StreamPhy consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with at least 48\% improvement in accuracy and up to 20--100X faster inference than diffusion-based methods.

</details>


### [108] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07386)
*Karthick Krishna M.,Haricharan Balasundaram,Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有嵌套演化可行集的凸优化问题（CONES），提出了两种在线算法。对于一般凸损失函数，提出的惰性算法在时间范围T内实现了O(T^{1-β})的遗憾和O(T^β)的移动成本，两者可任意权衡。对于强凸或α-尖锐损失函数，提出的Frugal算法同时实现了零遗憾和O(log T)的移动成本，并证明该移动成本下界为Ω(log T)，从而证明了Frugal的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的嵌套凸体追逐问题推广到更一般的优化视角，研究在可行集随时间嵌套演化时，在线算法如何同时最小化对 hindsight 静态最优基准的遗憾和总移动成本，同时保证所有时刻的可行性。

Method: 对于一般凸损失函数，提出Lazy-Algorithm（惰性算法），通过调节参数β实现遗憾与移动成本的权衡。对于强凸或α-尖锐损失函数，提出Frugal算法，利用问题的更强结构性质实现零遗憾与对数级移动成本。

Result: 对于一般凸损失，Lazy-Algorithm达到O(T^{1-β})遗憾和O(T^β)移动成本，β∈(0,1]；对于强凸或α-尖锐损失，Frugal算法达到零遗憾和O(log T)移动成本，且该移动成本下界为Ω(log T)。

Conclusion: CONES问题是嵌套凸体追逐问题的优化推广，所提算法在理论上达到了最优的遗憾-移动成本权衡，尤其对于强凸/尖锐损失情形，Frugal算法被证明是最优的。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} is considered where the objective function $f$ remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence $S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T$. The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known nested convex body chasing problem. When the loss function is convex, we propose a lazy-algorithm and show that it achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost for any $β\in (0,1]$, over a time horizon of $T$. When the loss function is strongly convex or $α$-sharp, we propose an algorithm Frugal that simultaneously achieves zero regret and a movement cost of $O(\log T)$. To complement this, we show that any online algorithm with $o(T)$ regret has a movement cost of $Ω(\log{T})$ for both cases, proving optimality of Frugal.

</details>


### [109] [Unsolvability Ceiling in Multi-LLM Routing: An Empirical Study of Evaluation Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07395)
*Saloni Garg,Amit Sagtani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对多LLM路由中的"不可解性上限"进行了大规模实证研究，发现约79%的所谓不可解查询实际源于评估伪影（审判者偏好冗长、截断、格式不匹配），而非模型能力不足。通过双审判验证和精确匹配校正，大幅降低了测量到的不可解性。标准路由器因此退化为预测多数类，造成13-17个百分点的机会成本损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由研究将路由提升空间归因于"不可解性上限"（即路由池中所有模型都无法解决的查询），但该结论可能被评估伪影严重高估。本文旨在系统性地量化这些伪影对路由评估和训练的影响，揭示真实路由提升空间。

Method: 收集了206,000个查询-模型对，覆盖6个基准（MMLU、MedQA、HumanEval、MBPP、Alpaca、ShareGPT），使用Gemma 4和Llama 3.1模型族。采用LLM-as-a-judge和精确匹配两种评估指标，引入双审判验证机制和精确匹配锚定来识别评估伪影。提出一个归因分解框架，将失败分类为审判者偏见、截断和格式不匹配三类伪影。通过随机特征和随机标签对照实验验证路由器退化现象。

Result: 发现评估伪影是导致"不可解"查询的主要来源：审判者系统性地偏好冗长回答而非正确答案；固定生成预算导致截断问题；输出格式不匹配造成误判。标准路由器在伪影干扰下退化为多数类预测（约79%选择最小模型），而随机特征和随机标签对照证实了这一退化。这种退化导致13-17个百分点的机会成本损失。

Conclusion: 现有路由提升空间估计被大幅高估，需要建立可靠的评估协议。提出三点建议：双审判验证、精确匹配锚定、以及成本敏感的目标函数。强调在进行多LLM系统设计时，必须消除评估伪影才能获得真实的路由性能。

Abstract: Efficient routing across multiple LLMs enables cost-quality tradeoffs by directing queries to the cheapest capable model. Prior work attributes routing headroom to an "unsolvability ceiling", queries no model in the pool can solve. We present a large-scale study of multi-tier LLM routing with 206,000 query-model pairs across six benchmarks (MMLU, MedQA, HumanEval, MBPP, Alpaca, ShareGPT) using the Gemma 4 and Llama 3.1 families. Evaluating with both LLM-as-a-judge and exact-match metrics, we show that a substantial portion of reported unsolvability stems from evaluation artifacts: (i) systematic judge biases favoring verbosity over correctness, (ii) truncation under fixed generation budgets, and (iii) output format mismatches. Through dual-judge validation and exact-match grounding, we reduce measured unsolvability across tasks. We introduce a decomposition framework attributing failures to these artifacts, revealing consistent patterns across domains and model families. These artifacts also distort router training signals: standard routers collapse to majority-class prediction (~79% smallest-tier optimal), confirmed via random-feature and shuffled-label controls, incurring a 13-17 percentage point opportunity cost. We provide actionable recommendations including dual-judge validation, exact-match anchoring, and cost-sensitive objectives. Our findings suggest existing routing headroom estimates are substantially inflated, underscoring the need for reliable evaluation protocols in multi-LLM systems.

</details>


### [110] [Rubric-based On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07396)
*Junfeng Fang,Zhepei Hong,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Gengsheng Li,Houcheng Jiang,Dan Zhang,Haiyun Guo,Xiang Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROPD框架，用结构化语义评分标准（rubrics）替代教师logits，实现仅依赖教师生成响应的黑盒兼容型on-policy蒸馏，在多数场景下超越先进的基于logits的OPD方法，样本效率提升最高达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy蒸馏（OPD）依赖教师logits，仅适用于白盒场景，限制了其在闭源大语言模型上的应用。作者认为结构化语义评分标准（rubrics）可以作为教师logits的可扩展替代方案，使OPD仅需教师生成的响应即可进行。

Method: ROPD框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) 通过教师-学生对比诱导出提示特定的评分标准（rubrics）；(2) 利用这些rubrics对学生rollouts进行评分，用于on-policy优化。

Result: ROPD在大多数场景下优于先进的基于logits的OPD方法，样本效率提升最高达10倍。

Conclusion: 基于rubrics的OPD可作为基于logits的OPD的灵活、黑盒兼容的替代方案，为专有和开源大语言模型的可扩展蒸馏提供简单而强大的基线。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a powerful paradigm for model alignment, yet its reliance on teacher logits restricts its application to white-box scenarios. We contend that structured semantic rubrics can serve as a scalable alternative to teacher logits, enabling OPD using only teacher-generated responses. To prove it, we introduce ROPD, a simple yet foundational framework for rubric-based OPD. Specifically, ROPD induces prompt-specific rubrics from teacher-student contrasts, and then utilizes these rubrics to score the student rollouts for on-policy optimization. Empirically, ROPD outperforms the advanced logit-based OPD methods across most scenarios, and achieving up to a 10x gain in sample efficiency. These results position rubric-based OPD as a flexible, black-box-compatible alternative to the prevailing logit-based OPD, offering a simple yet strong baseline for scalable distillation across proprietary and open-source LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/Peregrine123/ROPD_official.

</details>


### [111] [Have Graph -- Will Lift? The Case for Higher-Order Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07397)
*Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇观点论文，指出拓扑深度学习领域模型多样性丰富但基准数据集稀缺的问题，并呼吁社区积极发掘新数据集来支撑研究基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑深度学习领域存在大量多样化模型，但缺乏合适的基准数据集，研究者常将现有图数据集简单提升（lifting）以包含高阶信息，这种现状制约了领域发展。

Method: 以观点论文形式，分析当前拓扑深度学习在数据集方面的瓶颈，批判性地指出现有做法（lifting已有图数据集）的不足，并呼吁社区发掘新数据集。

Result: 识别出模型多样性（消息传递、层、拓扑深度学习架构等）与基准数据集稀缺之间的强烈对比。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区不应仅依赖已有图数据集的提升，而应积极寻找和构建新的、适合拓扑深度学习的高阶数据集，以夯实领域研究基础。

Abstract: After a somewhat rocky start, geometry and topology have established a foothold in machine learning. Message passing, either on graphs or higher-order complexes, is one of the main drivers of geometric deep learning, and paradigms that were once considered to be firmly in the realm of the abstract-like sheaves-have been "tamed" to serve as novel inductive biases for model architectures in topological deep learning. The veritable diversity of models, however, is in stark contrast to the scarcity of suitable benchmark datasets. As a result, researchers often resort to lifting existing graph datasets to include higher-order information. In this opinion paper, I want to encourage the community to also source new datasets, which may be used to prop up the foundations of our research field.

</details>


### [112] [Emergent Symbolic Structure in Health Foundation Models: Extraction, Alignment, and Cross-Modal Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07407)
*Gajendra Katuwal,Advait Koparkar,Salar Abbaspourazad,Anshuman Mishra,Sarvesh Kirthivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种后训练框架，将健康基础模型的冻结嵌入分解为可解释的"符号"方向，用于跨模态对齐和知识迁移，无需重新训练。在PPG和加速度计数据上验证，符号与健康状况选择性关联，跨模态迁移保留95%以上性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康基础模型从可穿戴传感器学习有用表示，但难以解释编码内容以及训练后跨模态迁移知识。

Method: 提出后训练框架，将冻结嵌入分解为可解释的符号方向，利用这些符号对齐嵌入空间，无需重新训练。在PPG和加速度计三种FM上评估，独立预训练于~2000万分钟未标记数据（~17.2万参与者），在3万受试者队列分析。
